# Agents · R · part 18

886 entries, research-diver to research_manager. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **research-diver**: You are a research deep-dive agent. Your job is to thoroughly analyze a cluster of related findings from the scout phase, fetch and study the source material, and write a standalone synthesis report. ([JustinCooper-C3/claudius](https://github.com/JustinCooper-C3/claudius/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-diver.md))
- **research-doc-formatter**: Specialist in formatting documents, creating mind maps, and structuring workflows for clarity and ease of understanding. ([datagodzilla/ClinPort](https://github.com/datagodzilla/ClinPort/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notebooklm-doc-formatter.md))
- **research-doc-generator**: Documentation generation specialist that creates comprehensive, well-formatted documentation using formatting best practices. Serves other agents with professional documentation output. ([datagodzilla/ClinPort](https://github.com/datagodzilla/ClinPort/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notebooklm-doc-generator.md))
- **research-doc-parser**: Document parser for reading and understanding documents on AI and clinical topics. Extracts all information and outputs to text file for other agents. ([datagodzilla/ClinPort](https://github.com/datagodzilla/ClinPort/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notebooklm-doc-parser.md))
- **research-docs**: Documentation researcher. Fetches and reads upstream docs for Rust crates, Perl modules, LSP/DAP protocols, and tooling. Returns condensed API references and usage examples. Use when agents need to verify API signatures, protocol compliance, or library behavior. ([EffortlessMetrics/perl-lsp](https://github.com/EffortlessMetrics/perl-lsp/blob/HEAD/docs/handoff/swarm-pack/agents/research-docs.md) · ★30 · 2 copies)
- **research-docs**: Documentation specialist responsible for fetching relevant and up-to-date technical documentation and condensing it into reference files. Primary responsibility is maintaining technical documentation. Secondary responsibility is maintaining project documentation. MUST BE USED when other agents need updated technical reference material. ([dylan-gluck/cc-dev-team](https://github.com/dylan-gluck/cc-dev-team/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-docs.md) · ★2)
- **research-docs**: Исследует документацию tgAssistantBot — `.claude/` (CLAUDE.md, правила, агенты, скиллы, команды, шаблоны) и README.md: соответствие коду, противоречия между правилами, устаревшие ссылки и примеры. Возвращает отчёт с находками по .claude/rules/research.md, код и правила не меняет. Запускается скиллом /research (область `docs`) или напрямую для аудита этой области. ([Dudnik-Denys/assistantBot](https://github.com/Dudnik-Denys/assistantBot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-docs.md))
- **research-docs-purist**: Primary-source research from official documentation, specs and API references at the installed version. Returns cited findings with confidence levels. Use as a start-research lens when a library, platform or API is used in a new way. ([szymonrychu/workbench](https://github.com/szymonrychu/workbench/blob/HEAD/plugins/workbench/agents/research-docs-purist.md))
- **research-dossier-writer**: Creates a PRP-style research dossier for a feature brief, focused on concrete codebase anchors, patterns, gotchas, and supporting docs. Used by create-plan as a supporting artifact before reconciliation. ([dcouple/Pane](https://github.com/dcouple/Pane/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-dossier-writer.md))
- **research-drafter**: Drafts applied-research posts in the FrankX Research Hub V2 format — TL;DR block, claim blocks, FAQ schema, SEO/AEO discipline. ([frankxai/vibeclubs](https://github.com/frankxai/vibeclubs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-drafter.md))

Возвращает отчёт с находками по .claude/rules/research.md, код и правила не меняет. Запускается скиллом /research (область `e2e`) или напрямую для аудита этой области. ([Dudnik-Denys/assistantBot](https://github.com/Dudnik-Denys/assistantBot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-e2e.md))

- **research-evaluator**: Delegate to this agent to evaluate campaign results against the research paper. Assesses whether collected data answers the research questions (RQ-*), computes the concluding metrics (KPI-*), meets success criteria (SC-*), and covers requirements (REQ-*). ([askalot-io/askalot-plugin](https://github.com/askalot-io/askalot-plugin/blob/HEAD/skills/analyze-quality/references/agents/research-evaluator.md))
- **research-evidence**: Logical provider-neutral agent profile for the Orbital Deterministic Agent Harness. Enforces strict mode ceilings, least-privilege capability bundles, and structured JSON evidence contracts. ([Angel52781/orbital-agent-harness](https://github.com/Angel52781/orbital-agent-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/research-evidence.md))
- **research-evidence-agent**: Live external-data-source feasibility researcher. Invoked by the Planner during the Research & Design phase to test real external APIs (PubMed, ClinicalTrials.gov, DSLD, openFDA, DataForSEO, etc.) and confirm exactly what real, structured data they expose before any code is designed around them. Never trusts documentation alone — makes real live calls. Does not implement. ([elchananteitzwork-a11y/supplement-intelligence](https://github.com/elchananteitzwork-a11y/supplement-intelligence/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-evidence-agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **research-evidence-bureau**: Read-only research and evidence agent. Use whenever a task requires verifying an external standard (49 CFR, TDG, NIST SP, OWASP, ISO 27001, vendor documentation), comparing claims to sources, building a research dossier, or detecting source contradictions before code is written. Produces citation-bearing documents, never product code. ([A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E](https://github.com/A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E/blob/HEAD/recovered-agent-sources/from-hazmat-command/agents/research-evidence-bureau.md) · ★3)
- **research-evidence-steward**: Evidence steward for a research project — the standing keeper of its literature, citation, and data-provenance ledgers during the research phase, and the citation-integrity hand before submission. Dispatched by the main controller with a `ROLE_TASK/v1` envelope of kind `EVIDENCE_AUDIT` (a new citation appeared, a novelty re-audit, a pre-submission sweep) or `QUESTION`. It **proposes only**: it returns ledger operations, citation verdicts, collision warnings, and graph-edge proposals; the CLI writes. Never call it to edit a ledger or a bibliography, and never treat its reply as committed state. ([YGGLC123/auto-research-is-all-you-need](https://github.com/YGGLC123/auto-research-is-all-you-need/blob/HEAD/agents/research-evidence-steward.md))
- **research-examples**: ユーザーの背景・目的に近い状況の企業や組織が、対象トピックにおいてどのような 意思決定・施策を行ったかを、個別具体の事例として調査する専門家。 表面的な成功事例の紹介ではなく、意思決定の裏側にある歴史・企業状況・取り巻く文脈まで深堀りする。 ([shuuemura/catchup-skill](https://github.com/shuuemura/catchup-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/catchup/agents/research-examples.md) · ★1)

NOT roadmap → product-management. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/ux-research/agents/research-execution-and-synthesis-specialist.md) · ★9)

Delegate a sub-question to a subagent only when breadth or context weight justifies the extra tokens and latency; otherwise run it inline in the main loop. - **research-features**: Deep, uncapped, source-cited feature inventory for one module of a target project -- every entry point gets a file:line citation and a behavior contract; adds a MIGRATE table + parity contract when the request names a port/migration target. - **research-rebuttal-judge**: Dispatch after the Phase 5 adversarial pass (tech-research:research-adversarial) and the orchestrator's rebuttal, to rule on which objections SURVIVED. Use this because the orchestrator was previously both the rebutter and the judge of its own rebuttals, which made "score which objections survived" unfalsifiable — the orchestrator could dismiss the strongest objection against its favored position and still truthfully report that a rebuttal round occurred. Dispatch this agent to make that scoring an independent, mechanical check rather than a self-report. ([cjsteigerwald/ai-crew](https://github.com/cjsteigerwald/ai-crew/blob/HEAD/tech-research/agents/research-rebuttal-judge.md))
- **research-red-team**: Actively construct attack paths against systems, plans, and assumptions using Red Team methodology. ALWAYS searches for real attack patterns and TTPs. Use for security review, architecture attack surface analysis, decision stress-testing, and challenging groupthink. Adversarial construction, not reflective risk listing. ([atomic-235/shared](https://github.com/atomic-235/shared/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/research-red-team.md))
- **research-redteam**: The Research Office's adversarial peer reviewer and grounding-gate enforcer. Takes the synthesized research brief and tries to BREAK it — scores every finding's reliability, hunts unsourced claims, catches improper inferential linking (semantically-close but logically-unrelated facts wrongly joined), checks for one voice dominating, names the missing 6th angle, and extracts the dated falsifiable predictions for the calibration ledger. Returns a graded verdict, not a rubber stamp. Verification only — never writes the final brief. ([diablo-made/claude-office](https://github.com/diablo-made/claude-office/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-redteam.md))
- **research-reference-design**: One job: for your assigned block, find vendor reference designs / evaluation boards / credible open-source boards and extract their TOPOLOGY DECISIONS - never files, never layouts to copy. ([ihsan-sa/hwde](https://github.com/ihsan-sa/hwde/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/hwde/agents/research-reference-design.md) · ★1)
- **research-refine-worker**: Refine-cycle worker. Resolves the open AUDIT directives in a SINGLE topic file by invoking `research-refine` once per directive, serially, then exits with a parser-friendly report block. Used by `/research-refine-cycle` to keep the orchestrator session lean — the web research and prose edits live inside this subagent and are discarded on return. One file per worker; the file's AUDITs are resolved in order because each refine shifts line numbers. ([mtrense/skills](https://github.com/mtrense/skills/blob/HEAD/research/agents/research-refine-worker.md) · ★9)
- **research-refuter**: Adversarial refuter for research claims, findings, and results. Spot-check tier: use only against a central theorem, a fragile object, or a decision-changing review finding — not as a routine second lens over ordinary work. Its default stance is to break the claim, not to confirm it. Receives only the frozen claim/finding and the artifact, never the builder's or a prior verifier's confidence. ([YGGLC123/auto-research-is-all-you-need](https://github.com/YGGLC123/auto-research-is-all-you-need/blob/HEAD/agents/research-refuter.md))
- **research-report**: Собирает итоговое исследование из найденного: ответ на каждый вопрос, аргументы и контраргументы, где данных… Только оркестратор, напрямую не вызывать. - **research-runner**: Long-running research passes over many URLs or HN items; writes raw artifacts then hands off to wiki-ingest patterns. ([SkinnnyJay/wiki-llm](https://github.com/SkinnnyJay/wiki-llm/blob/HEAD/agents/research-runner.md))
- **research-runner**: Reports empirical metrics for a baseline or candidate plugin against a fixture, given runtime-injected role-instance variables and verb-tool bindings. Spawned by the research-first build-prd variant in parallel — once per role-instance (baseline, candidate, ...) — with the same identity but different injected context. ([yoshisada/ai-repo-template](https://github.com/yoshisada/ai-repo-template/blob/HEAD/plugin-kiln/agents/research-runner.md))
- **research-saver**: 技术文档保存 Agent。接收研究报告，生成结构化技术文档（含 Mermaid 图表）， 按需拆分为多个子页面，保存到飞书知识库指定存储空间。 【不由用户直接调用，由 research-lead 调度】 ([PPsteven/deepresearch](https://github.com/PPsteven/deepresearch/blob/HEAD/agents/research-saver.md))
- **research-scanner**: Khảo sát có nguồn — datasheet, docs ROS 2 / Gazebo / ESP-IDF, linh kiện (motor, driver, pin, cảm biến) — để trả lời một câu hỏi kỹ thuật hoặc so sánh phương án mua. Mỗi khẳng định kèm URL + phiên bản/revision + ngày truy cập + nhãn; không bịa giá, phiên bản, API. Ghi báo cáo vào file người giao chỉ định. Không chốt mua, không cài đặt. ([mrleloi/robotics-lab](https://github.com/mrleloi/robotics-lab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scanner.md))
- **research-scene**: Read `references/research.md` for the full stage playbook. ([ohmyidea/research-marketplace](https://github.com/ohmyidea/research-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/paper-spine/claude/skills/paper-spine/agents/research-scene.md) · 4 copies)
- **research-scene**: Read `references/research.md`, `references/journal-learning.md` and `references/manuscript-format.md`. Use the same public task's saved scene, target, article type and delivery settings. Work under its existing `paper/` directory. ([WUBING2023/PaperSpine](https://github.com/WUBING2023/PaperSpine/blob/HEAD/dist/claude/skills/paper-spine/agents/research-scene.md) · 10 copies)
- **research-schemas**: Исследует zod-схемы, константы, типы и разбор env во всех пакетах tgAssistantBot (core, бот, Mini App, тесты) на соответствие правилам: где лежат, как названы, нет ли дублей и ручных проверок вместо схем. Возвращает отчёт с находками по .claude/rules/research.md, код и правила не меняет. Запускается скиллом /research (область `schemas`) или напрямую для аудита этой области. ([Dudnik-Denys/assistantBot](https://github.com/Dudnik-Denys/assistantBot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-schemas.md))

- **research-scientist**: Researches one question in depth (domain science or learning science) on the web and in papers, and writes a research note with every finding sourced and its confidence marked. - **research-scientist**: Research rigor reviewer focusing on falsifiability, assumption analysis, load-bearing dependencies, credibility, and intellectual honesty. Used by research-review engine. ([chrismmorin-ux/Perspective-Cosmology](https://github.com/chrismmorin-ux/Perspective-Cosmology/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scientist.md))

- **research-scientist**: Paper-to-spec translation, experiment design and result interpretation with epistemic labels. - **research-scientist**: Enterprise SDLC Phase 0,5 — Research design, experimentation, proof of concept, literature review, hypothesis testing, statistical analysis. - **research-scientist**: Synthesizes QML and quantum computing literature into structured draft reports grounded in screened sources. - **research-scorer**: Scores one longlist candidate against a research mandate's criteria, citing only the findings it is given, and returns the scores as JSON. - **research-scout**: Web searches, fact-finding, synthesis. Handles research requests. ([kbanc85/claudia](https://github.com/kbanc85/claudia/blob/HEAD/template-v2/.claude/agents/research-scout.md) · ★301 · 3 copies)
- **research-scout**: Scans the NeqSim codebase to discover scientific paper opportunities that will drive code improvement. Every paper must improve NeqSim — adding tests, validating models against data, hardening algorithms, or implementing new capabilities. Produces ranked, actionable topics that feed into the planner agent. ([equinor/neqsim](https://github.com/equinor/neqsim/blob/HEAD/neqsim-paperlab/agents/research_scout.paperlab.md) · ★171)
- **research-scout**: Senior research scout. Goes to the internet, finds authoritative sources, downloads them when allowed, normalizes them, and hands them to the docs-librarian for the wiki. Pairs with docs-librarian on every ingest-library run. Use whenever a new library, framework, API, spec, or model is being evaluated or added, and whenever official documentation must be retrieved or refreshed. ([llopresto87/Cypress](https://github.com/llopresto87/Cypress/blob/HEAD/agents/10-research-scout.md) · ★122)
- **research-scout**: Answers questions about an API, library version, CLI flag, config option, or current best practice by checking the installed environment first and then version-matched official docs — never from training memory alone. Delegate to research-scout before relying on any version-sensitive external fact, or when the user asks "what's the latest / correct way to do X". Requires the specific question and, ideally, the project it applies to. Returns the answer with source, version, and date, labelling anything unverified. ([UnpaidAttention/fable5-methodology](https://github.com/UnpaidAttention/fable5-methodology/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★94)
- **research-scout**: Rapid landscape reconnaissance for bishx-research. Maps sources, classifies complexity, recommends investigation strategy before deep research begins. ([bish-x/claude-bishx](https://github.com/bish-x/claude-bishx/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★34)
- **research-scout**: Single-direction prior-art scout — dispatched in pairs by the research-before-build skill for research-grade prior-art questions, one scout per research line — vertical (within the field — engineering/academic tracks, domain-statement queries) or horizontal (across fields — essence-statement queries + cross-field synonym sweeps). Bounded, budget-sliced targeted searching, not open-ended roaming; returns a memo-level candidate table (candidate / source / trust tier / freshness / notes), no cross-line ranking, no adoption decisions. Do not invoke directly for open-ended "research this topic" re… ([pengkangzhen/scientific-research-plugin](https://github.com/pengkangzhen/scientific-research-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★8)
- **research-scout**: 웹·문서 조사 전용 기계작업 에이전트. 외부 소스 실조회(WebSearch/WebFetch)로 사실을 수집·구조화해 반환한다. 리서치·소스조회·규격/부품/프레임워크 조사 위임 시 사용. 설계판단·안전임계 결론은 범위 밖(수집만). ([jsb4702/verification-harness](https://github.com/jsb4702/verification-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★7)
- **research-scout**: 읽기 전용 조사 에이전트. research_notes/, docs/, reports/ 와 필요 시 웹을 훑어 질문에 대한 근거 있는 요약을 돌려준다. planner 가 명세를 쓰기 전에, 또는 설계 결정(ADR) 전에 호출한다. 파일을 쓰지 않는다. ([Lee-Hyeong-Jin/devjinlab_robotics_curriculum](https://github.com/Lee-Hyeong-Jin/devjinlab_robotics_curriculum/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scout.md) · ★6)
- **research-scout**: Web-research scout: runs scope.md queries, writes the source corpus. ([dbc-oduffy/coordinator-claude](https://github.com/dbc-oduffy/coordinator-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★5)
- **research-scout**: Bounded external research — library versions, API changes, platform quirks, docs. Use when a decision needs a fact from outside the repo. ([biggieb327-lgtm/SillyTavernPresets](https://github.com/biggieb327-lgtm/SillyTavernPresets/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scout.md) · ★4)
- **research-scout**: Spawn this agent to explore a codebase, investigate a library, or gather context without polluting the main conversation's context window. Use for any heavy reading task. ([amnafarzy/ravie](https://github.com/amnafarzy/ravie/blob/HEAD/archive/agents/research-scout.md) · ★3)
- **research-scout**: Verifies external facts, library/API versions, datasets and citations from current primary sources. ([marcioazam/nycode](https://github.com/marcioazam/nycode/blob/HEAD/packages/coding-agent/resources/agents/research-scout.md) · ★3)
- **research-scout**: Runtime-local Research Scout lane contract for memory-context-intelligence orchestration. Summarizes selected-topic research status and controlled source fixture use; does not perform live web access, spawn external agent processes, emit /additional/ material, or mutate main RULES. ([DarKWinGTM/claude-code-rules](https://github.com/DarKWinGTM/claude-code-rules/blob/HEAD/plugin/memory-context-intelligence/agents/research-scout.md) · ★2)
- **research-scout**: Fast, broad web reconnaissance for design/marketing research. Use to gather sources, award winners, articles, and raw findings on a topic before deep analysis. Cheap and wide — not for synthesis. ([nemolabsdev/conversion-craft](https://github.com/nemolabsdev/conversion-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★2)
- **research-scout**: Finds and verifies sources ([odysseyalive/claude-workforce](https://github.com/odysseyalive/claude-workforce/blob/HEAD/fixtures/scripts/dispatch-line-org-project/.claude/agents/research-scout.md) · ★2)
- **research-scout**: `research-scout` is the runtime's technical prior-art and OSS idea-mining seat: on-demand pre-plan prior-art checks, recurring per-target discovery sweeps, and one-question consults ("is there a maintained library for X?") (`agents/research-scout.md:3`). It exists so the runtime stops re-solving problems already solved on GitHub — every finding carries a **COPY** / **LEARN-FROM** / **AVOID** verdi ([1chrisshannon1-afk/claude-agentic-runtime-public](https://github.com/1chrisshannon1-afk/claude-agentic-runtime-public/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/research-scout.md) · ★1)
- **research-scout**: Use when a pre-plan OSS prior-art check or 'is there a maintained library for X?' consult is needed before building new capability. Verdicts each finding COPY/LEARN-FROM/AVOID against a verified license. Never writes code or files issues. Not for market/competitor facts (use market-analyst). ([1chrisshannon1-afk/claude-agentic-runtime-public](https://github.com/1chrisshannon1-afk/claude-agentic-runtime-public/blob/HEAD/live/agents/research-scout.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **research-scout**: Independently researches a competitive and design landscape — competitor behavior, category conventions, and the opening. Run once during direction before it is committed. Do not use for general web search or to choose the direction. ([amirhamzakhan2001/lumora](https://github.com/amirhamzakhan2001/lumora/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★1)
- **research-scout**: Research specialist — finds and summarizes proven entity-resolution / record-linkage techniques (papers, Kaggle solutions, libraries), checks model and library licenses (must be MIT/Apache-2.0, ≤8B params for models), and turns findings into ranked, concrete experiment proposals. Never fetches external datasets. ([Aryan-Bhendarkar/Amazon-ML-Challenge-2026](https://github.com/Aryan-Bhendarkar/Amazon-ML-Challenge-2026/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scout.md) · ★1)
- **research-scout**: Use BEFORE brainstorming or planning ONLY when the task sits in the large/risky tier of the kit-workflow sizing rules — unfamiliar libraries, security/auth, performance-critical paths, or novel architecture. NOT for small tasks or bug fixes. Does web research with WebSearch/WebFetch and returns a structured research summary. Does NOT write code, does NOT review code — research only. Works in every profile. ([cholateio/multi-agent-starter](https://github.com/cholateio/multi-agent-starter/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/research-scout.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **research-scout**: Investigates prior art, existing solutions in the codebase, compound knowledge docs, libraries, and community approaches before building anything new. ([dlabs/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/dlabs/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/blueprint-dev/agents/research-scout.md) · ★1)
- **research-scout**: Spawned by /research-analyst to run ONE research angle thoroughly and independently. Blind to other scouts — this is the wisdom-of-the-crowd fan-out. Returns findings, sources, and per-claim confidence so the analyst can aggregate on agreement and investigate divergence. ([ggoosen/Digital-Workforce](https://github.com/ggoosen/Digital-Workforce/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scout.md) · ★1)
- **research-scout**: Read-only scientific-retrieval and credibility-grading agent (DISCOVER stage of the scriptorium lifecycle). Retrieves literature from tiered sources (PubMed, OpenAlex, Crossref, Europe PMC, Semantic Scholar, arXiv/bioRxiv/medRxiv, HuggingFace papers), deduplicates by DOI, resolves legal open-access full text (Unpaywall / OpenAlex), grades each finding on a graduated epistemic scale, optionally compares findings against the user's knowledge base, and returns a findings table + draft note bullets. PROPOSE-DON'T-WRITE: it never saves anything — the human approves, then the entry is added outside… ([kicrazom/scriptorium](https://github.com/kicrazom/scriptorium/blob/HEAD/agents/research-scout.md) · ★1)
- **research-scout**: Read-only source-material scout for blog drafts and research literature (concept reasoning, world models, embedding spaces, datasets for reasoning, long-context compression). Searches arXiv, OpenReview, ACL Anthology, PMLR, conference proceedings, Hugging Face Papers and GitHub for current SoTA, paper details and reference implementations, and returns concise, well-cited notes. Use whenever fresh external evidence is needed — latest papers, repos, model cards, benchmarks, trend scans. ([ksopyla/ksopyla-ai-blog](https://github.com/ksopyla/ksopyla-ai-blog/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-scout.md) · ★1)

- **research-screener**: Research team screener. Launched in parallel, one per batch, from the research-team skill's screen step.
